                    <tr>
                        <td style="padding: 28px 36px 18px 36px;">
                            <div style="font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; color: #0F172A; margin-bottom: 14px;">
                                💡 AI Executive Summary: Key News Insights
                            </div>
                            <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" width="100%" style="background-color: #F8FAFC; border-left: 4px solid #FF9900; border: 1px solid #E2E8F0; border-left-width: 4px;">
                                <tr>
                                    <td style="padding: 20px 24px; font-size: 13.5px; color: #334155; line-height: 1.7em; text-align: justify;">
                                        글로벌 빅테크의 AI 인프라 투자가 자본 확충과 재무 리스크 관리의 균형점을 모색하는 국면에 진입함. 엔비디아가 오픈AI 데이터센터 채무 보증 규모를 2,500억 달러에서 1,200억 달러 미만으로 절반 축소하며 재무 레버리지 관리에 나선 반면 <a href='https://www.chosun.com/economy/tech_it/2026/08/15/SFODZQT2YFEF7GFT527INR6WUA/' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[40]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMipwFBVV95cUxNa2dNNzBhM1pEcGdMRXVRN1VObHJoWnFPYy16TFAtNDF6ZkxoMW12dy1kbVNjRXY5TkVlMFY3ZDJfTGhuaXM1cUpnV2ZwUVA4X0trTEVsLVQyWlNiSEhhem9CTVZ4bS1RYUZCNTB3ZHBFQk9sM1NWVVBDYnVEaDhwaEE4a2tUSTlKUklBTGg5cHRRWkZQaHhIS2RUVUFXM203cUVjWGZtSQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[6]</a>, 오픈AI 전력 확보를 위해 SB 에너지에 30억 달러 투자를 검토하며 직접 공급망을 틀어쥐는 전략을 취함 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Nvidia eyes investing $3 billion in SoftBank's SB Energy under OpenAI data center deal, Information says&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[4]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiiAFBVV95cUxPS19pTjhFcnhRaEM4djRkVml0RmlrS1hjeXl2ckZzUFBPaUFIRTJzYlRGTm1QclZ3Ym44aEhrZ1JzdUF0VFJxVTdlWHY0bkJwbW1DclM1TXpkejhhOFJwaVVReUNBN0Ztc2xoWVhyVWFVQU5vQWZWczMxdk1IQUhXN01nNjdoOTdx?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[26]</a>. 동시에 AMD가 50억 달러 규모 자금 조달에 나서고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMibkFVX3lxTE9Id25pQnFHQTduUWpSV1hhQjRlRUxnb0lNQWxGalc4dlE0NzBFb0hjamloQzkwX3RxZ1NVYy04STNTMTdsdDdaZ0Z3RXRVeTdzN2N3MTRESVJzY1dCeE80anNBY3dCQ3dCMUFRT2JB?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[29]</a> xAI가 10GW 연산력 확보를 목표로 7배 증설을 선언하는 등 연산 용량 확충이 잇따르는 가운데 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Elon Musk says xAI will increase data center capacity 7x by 2027 targeting 10 gigawatts of compute, up to $500 billion in revenue by the end of next year&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[2]</a>, 유럽 CVC DIF의 퍼스트콜로 인수와 멕시코 KIO의 13억 달러 확장 등 글로벌 거점 확장이 지속됨 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMimgFBVV95cUxOT2xKZXI2U3NkQ2FVMzJnblpRT1ZsQ1BjeGZWcUdRem5qYUdHQnk1UGhkRzQ2ZkEtSEliMVZyNUFrWHNtVGZUc3FiNVBmM1gwSm81LWRrQy1zZ0p3Xzk2bUd4Szc1OVZ0V1Fra3hHTkZtcTlEMW5tZTUySjUxLVRra2R5QzBMdmJZa0R3WlJaZ3Y1RnI1eVFMM1BB?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[13]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMinwFBVV95cUxNajdGRERyWEdWM1FTTFJVbnpEbXRyTGdQRGNvdzRNRlhWR0xzNE1JMm5URVYyMnIxN1BxSDR2aVZNRmkzSk5JalB3c3FVcjBLY09SM1QxQWd2SzBvVjdlQWFnRHg0Q1BHdHVOWUQ5NEM4TDdTcWp5Q21rVjdBX3dVdERrcGlkN1JlMzRmclN2T0hOdk1wZk1aaTFaaTNtYkk?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[14]</a>. 국내에서도 충남대와 네이버클라우드의 중부권 AI 거점 구축과 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMia0FVX3lxTE5KWkEwMENrTDJCQzB1TFkzVVI5VXVBcnFxRUFVVzg3S3JNN0JLNWdTZVZGNEdUV2pKcWw3M0tnd1BPOFNid0I0QklhNVBmc3VaNURDcjRBYms0SV9iUE5VX3oxN2VRVmlHZVM0?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[24]</a> 피플바이오의 서울 삼성동 도심형 엣지 데이터센터 추진 등 거점 다변화가 가시화되는 양상임 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMid0FVX3lxTFBMa1I5OFprZ2ZtNUhRWG5IZ0RtcXZCUG91RktaS0VrVDNjQnRBN0tabXhsTTEzR0JSS00xQ1ZpRWRyMEN5NHk0ZjJxMFd0MUN5VkVRRF9TV0J0RTgtZE1ZQkJ1Rk1VR3FCZ25VZTl5SEE3SHB5UDdv?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[37]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiY0FVX3lxTE4zQ0tnTVhTb1dISHBtTmo2OHhpQWtHYTV4TXVHbGtReEc5OXRzbVZFb28zb0xacUNwOWRQUTBUNkhJbVhCU1JPYV9veFY3U3ItaHRKSTdnbVNJSmU5NFY3ME1ORQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[15]</a>.<BR><BR>AI 데이터센터의 기하급수적 전력 갈증을 해소하기 위해 차세대 원전(SMR) 및 기저전원 밸류체인 구축과 전력 기자재 수주전이 격화되는 추세임. 메타가 캐피털 파워와의 대형 전력 공급 계약과 더불어 오클로(Oklo)와 SMR 파트너십을 체결하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMioAFBVV95cUxNSmhrQWxhRFk1bVNsNzhrOHoyUmxXSmZMTUtCT241ZW9mMExSdm1VLXZKYVlSaUFkZy1yNTFRbEdtb3lYaEZLQnJha2JFX1UyVU9zQ1hBZnVwZUgteXVrd0swMXcwYVozbHI2VXZnWUo3Vy0yTW5mUW1DX2hMWVU5QXBCVklhc255YzBuTmxFbnhQbmNlQTlNZGxfMmkxcEZG0gGgAUFVX3lxTE1KaGtBbGFEWTVtU2w3OGs4ejJSbFdKZkxNS0JPbjVlb2YwTFJ2bVUtdkphWVJpQWRnLXI1MVFsR21veVhoRktCcmFrYkVfVTJVT3NDWEFmdXBlSC15dWt3SzAxdzBhWjNscjZVdmdZSjdXLTJNbmZRbUNfaExZVTlBcEJWSWFzbnljMG5ObEVueFBuY2VBOU1kbF8yaTFwRkY?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[16]</a> <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Nuclear Newcomer Oklo Partners with Meta to Power Data Centers, A Thirty-Year Marathon Tests Investor Patience&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[3]</a>, 컨스텔레이션이 920MW 규모의 장기 원전 공급 계약을 성사시키며 무탄소 기저전원 확보를 가속화함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZEFVX3lxTE8tQVVMdW9kWDAtY1UtUFJpS1V4VDBweGJacG83ZGpSYXYwX1VGaWl3aE1CLTFTbzZWbFpwOWZMYzZoRi1kRzZkUG5CODJsNEliTjZsNHhUWUtPWkUyOTgzaURlOHc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[34]</a>. 국내 산업계 역시 글로벌 원전 동맹을 적극 확장하며, SK이노베이션이 빌 게이츠의 테라파워와 소듐냉각고속로(SFR) 기반 나트륨 SMR 사업협력 합의서를 전격 체결해 글로벌 시장 공략 교두보를 마련함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMicEFVX3lxTE1ycEVqYXFBNXNWTS1TZkhWQm9CbmpmRjVPMkljRXRmcm5tRk9UdnVyTGZIMnZrNzdYVjRnNkNiU0lTN25WV3A5d1RRSVp5UU9HempzSXVrTUhrZTJEeFhBYnBxdEdOYmNraXRHaXpKaHE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[21]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMibEFVX3lxTFA0bXYwRGhRUm54eXpnNkdxUk5ndHphRVlJRjZsTGRlRFRMLXhab2gxSEtQdzU2OFRSZnJxZVlNYWlHbkFyVld2SzlwZEo0MUpuY25hWXZxb3VSVlJCMDBCNDVwb1huQWt1dWQtLQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[32]</a>. 아울러 HD현대중공업이 미국 데이터센터에 9,560억 원 규모 발전설비를 공급하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiakFVX3lxTE1IRWhfSVhwaVpIZHBHWGU3NUhvd2VyYjdtY0Z1bUpYLWphRlhKSHV2cnNpVlg1d1BnZUJpNzFScXFpclc4TF9GbThGY21fUTV1VFBvOU1HM1I5UWJxdk9DR244OVE1ZDJmMEE?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[19]</a>, LS그룹은 수주잔고 20조 원을 바탕으로 상반기 영업익 1조 원을 돌파하는 등 전력 슈퍼사이클의 최대 수혜를 입증함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiakFVX3lxTE1Sa3BIbHdQN2RacWlsNjVzRHZQQmt4Z3RfUzh4b1JOSFJadnhCaThfWnRZRmtOZjl0bUJ5WXhmdG5aZDlwWEpxOGZFeUtJOC1kdGVoYmVOWEo5WW9sdWhJbGY3M1pYdTQwOWc?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[27]</a> <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMib0FVX3lxTE9YdURENTNZUU5jZXJIcVp3d3pQUGpwb1kzQ1hkdUFLdjhTbHBWNUlRZ1otNG9uLXpGbjJ1d2RDVjJfYXpKR2tQMG9FQXdHeFJjbW9jTUFIeldQNEVPNHFVTy1nd2hMcENncE1EMFgxQQ?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[22]</a>. 또한 네이버가 풍력발전사 지분 30%를 인수하는 등 친환경 분산 전원 직접 확보도 다각화되는 형국임 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZEFVX3lxTE0xY3hEaklRTHhyTTZrcTZ3YmJDMTI4RGpoMms1cTBtYlNELTc1MnF4MmhXa1hONGFQeUhkYXFzclhiTTUwYUlHZktkQ0V4LTNwbFJFQ3NQYWtLX055ekZ4MExQMEY?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[23]</a>.<BR><BR>초고집적 AI 랙의 전력 손실을 줄이고 극한의 발열을 제어하기 위한 전력 아키텍처 혁신과 액체냉각 기술 전환이 산업 표준으로 자리 잡는 중임. 엔비디아가 랙 한 줄당 2MW급 연산력을 수용하는 800V DC(직류) 고전압 배전 아키텍처의 양산 도입을 공식화하면서 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiVkFVX3lxTFA4ZWkzdGM2SzNtU0NYUU8zNHZxd05oSVVENVhQOUxNWXpuVUE4aWNBRkJoNWRqWnNyNTE1RFVGVmc1SC1JY0pxd0gyTlFrQ3JKN3oxcl9B?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7;
